St. Christopher Medal of the Engineer st_christopher_medal_of_the_engineer · armor neck

Uncle Carl kept a horseshoe charm on it and swore the thing had paid for Universal City twice over. He has given it to whoever keeps the backlot standing longest; luck, he says, is wasted on a man indoors.

Vega Mechanic's Coveralls vega_mechanic_s_coveralls · armor coat

Oil-black at the knees and pocketed for every wrench in the Burbank shop. The man who wore them knew a Vega's engine by its sound alone, which is exactly why the hive decided to keep him.

Hive Queen's Chitin Crown hive_queen_s_chitin_crown · armor hat

Not a crown, strictly, but the hard ridge of the Mount Lee queen's brow, cut free and lined with felt by a Legion armourer who will not discuss it. It still hums when the hills go quiet.

Jagged helm of iridescent green-black chitin, tall spiked ridge crest, steel band at the rim, amber resin glowing in the seams.

Mantle of the Thirteen Letters mantle_of_the_thirteen_letters · armor coat

Woven on the ridge behind the sign from hive silk and sheet metal stripped off all thirteen letters. The realtors paid a fortune to light HOLLYWOODLAND at night; the mantle still flickers faintly, as if remembering the bill.

Draped cloak of pale shimmering hive silk stitched with white sheet-metal scales, tiny bulbs glowing along the hem, collar clasped with amber resin.

Aviatrix's Flight Jacket aviatrix_s_flight_jacket · armor coat

Women flew out of United farther and faster than the newsreels admitted. This jacket came back from the hangars with a torn sleeve and a cracked compass in the pocket, and the women at the field still keep its owner's seat.

Brown horsehide flight jacket, wide fleece collar, zip front, small brass compass pinned at the breast, one sleeve torn and resin-stained.

Hangar Mother's Carapace hangar_mother_s_carapace · armor coat

Something in the United hangars laid its brood in the cabins of grounded airliners. Its back plate, riveted to a steel vest by men who had flown with nothing beneath them but air, turns a bullet the way a hangar door turns rain.

Massive chest plate of curved green-black chitin bolted onto a steel vest, corrugated aluminium side panels, brood pores glowing amber.

Architect's Drafting Gloves architect_s_drafting_gloves · armor gloves

Fine kid gloves smudged with graphite, recovered from the hangar office where the hive laid out its tunnels in pencil on real blueprints. Someone up there taught them to draw. Nobody wants to learn who.

Pair of slim grey kid gloves, graphite-smudged fingertips, a brass drafting compass tucked in one cuff, faint amber resin lines traced across the palm.

King Consort's Greaves king_consort_s_greaves · armor trousers

Her Majesty's consort guarded the chamber for years and was never once asked his opinion, a position familiar to several husbands on the Boulevard. His leg shells, strapped over steel, hold firm when the mountain shakes.

Steel leg greaves overlaid with long ribbed chitin plates, iridescent green-black, jointed knee caps, worn leather straps and brass buckles.

Trimotor Pilot's Boots trimotor_pilot_s_boots · armor shoes

Fleece-lined for the cold over the Tehachapis, back when there was somewhere to fly. The pilot left them on the rudder pedals of a Ford Trimotor in the end hangar; the plane is still there, full of eggs.

Tall padded flying boots with quilted canvas uppers, sheepskin cuffs folded down, thick rubber soles, one heel crusted with amber egg resin.

Heart Chamber Signet heart_chamber_signet · armor ring

Pried from the wall of the innermost chamber on Mount Lee, where the resin pulses slowly like a sleeper breathing. Preachers wear it and swear the ring prays along with them. Nobody has asked to whom.

Heavy gold signet ring set with a domed oval of glowing amber resin, a dark insect shape suspended inside, chitin shoulders on the band.

Beery's Barnstormer Scarf beery_s_barnstormer_scarf · armor neck

Wallace Beery flew his own plane and described it at length to anyone at the airport lunch counter. He gave this scarf to the first crew back from the hangars, asked for it back, then thought better of it.

Long white silk aviator scarf with fringed ends, light oil smudges, a small gilt propeller pin fastened near one end.

Runway Queen's Sash runway_queen_s_sash · armor belt

Its first owner won the airport's opening-day pageant and wore it for one afternoon. The hive moved into the hangars soon after, and the sash went to work; backed in tooled leather, it holds a holster better than it ever held a title.

Wide royal-blue satin pageant sash backed with tooled tan leather, brass buckle, gilt winged emblem, small holster loop at the side.

Deacon's Revival Vestments deacon_s_revival_vestments · armor coat

Stitched for the tent revivals Angelus Temple carried into the hills, heavy enough to stop a spitter's acid and plain enough to satisfy the deacons. The collar still smells of lamp oil and five thousand hymnals.

Long dark leather preacher's coat, white satin lapels, gilt cross embroidered on the breast, brass buttons, frayed red hymnal ribbon hanging from a pocket.

Chorus Conductor's Cuffs chorus_conductor_s_cuffs · armor wrist

Starched linen worn by the choirmaster at the Hollywood Bowl's Easter sunrise service. He kept conducting the night the swarm came over the Pass, and the hymn held just long enough for the crowd to reach the road.

Pair of starched white linen cuffs, gilt lyre-shaped cufflinks, silver thread trim, a faint amber resin smear on one edge.

Usher's Flashlight Baton usher_s_flashlight_baton · weapon main

Grauman's ushers were drilled like Marines, and this one cleared a Drone from the Egyptian's forecourt with the same nickel-plated torch he used to find patrons their seats. The bulb still works, which is more than the Drone can say.

Long nickel-plated flashlight baton, ribbed black rubber grip, red velvet wrist cord with a gold tassel, glowing amber lens at the tip, small dent near the head.

Hive Warden's Mandible hive_warden_s_mandible · weapon main

Pried from the thing that guarded the hive chamber under the Egyptian. The jaw is serrated like a bread knife and twice as hungry, and somebody has wrapped the hinge in friction tape and called it a handle.

Curved serrated insect mandible used as a knife, glossy green-black chitin blade with jagged teeth, hinge end wrapped in black friction tape, faint amber resin at the base.

Egyptian Prop Khopesh egyptian_prop_khopesh · weapon main

Carved for a stage prologue in 1922 and sharpened for the first time in 1930. The gilt paint flakes off in the scabbard; the edge, somebody discovered the hard way, does not.

Sickle-curved khopesh sword, flaking gold-painted blade showing bare grey steel along the honed edge, turquoise and lapis painted hilt, small scarab pommel.

Projection Booth Arc Lamp projection_booth_arc_lamp · weapon off

Carbon rods hiss inside the housing the way they did through every reel of the opening run. The projectionist claimed it could throw a picture through cigar smoke and a bad review.

Compact black iron projector arc lamp housing with a round brass-ringed lens, cooling fins on top, carry handle, intense white-violet glow leaking from vent slots.

Matriarch's Spitting Gland Sprayer matriarch_s_spitting_gland_sprayer · weapon ranged

A Colt frame married to an invader gland by a stagehand with steady nerves and no medical training. It fires what the Matriarch used to spit, and it smells exactly like it.

Blued revolver frame with a bulbous translucent green gland strapped above the barrel by copper wire, rubber tube feeding the muzzle, dripping acid-green bead at the tip.

Stage Brace stage_brace · weapon main

Every flat on the Egyptian's stage leaned on a few of these hardwood braces. Unbolted from the scenery, it props up a fight just as well, though the set has never quite stood straight since.

Long hardwood stage brace pole, pale oak with an iron hook at one end and a heavy cast-iron foot plate at the other, splashes of painted-flat blue.

Lighting Rigger's Wrench lighting_rigger_s_wrench · weapon main

Its owner tightened clamps forty feet above the stage and never once dropped a tool on an actor, a record he considered the finest achievement in pictures.

Heavy adjustable spanner wrench, dark steel with worn red-enamel handle, coil of safety cord tied through the grip hole, small brass lamp-clamp charm dangling.

Robin Hood's Prop Quarterstaff robin_hood_s_prop_quarterstaff · weapon main

Douglas Fairbanks's Robin Hood opened the Egyptian in 1922, and this staff came out of the storage room still waiting for its cue. Light enough to vault with, which the stuntmen did, and heavy enough to matter.

Tall straight ash quarterstaff, honey-brown wood, green leather grip wrapping in the middle, iron-capped ends, a single small feather tied below one cap.

Sexton's Spade sexton_s_spade · weapon main

Hollywood Cemetery's old sexton kept the edge keen and the handle oiled through forty years of quiet work. Since the crypts went bad the work has been noisier, and the spade has had to dig faster.

Long-handled gravedigger's spade, worn dark ash handle with a D-grip, broad iron blade crusted with dry grey earth, small tarnished brass ring on the shaft.

Stembridge Rental Thompson stembridge_rental_thompson · weapon ranged

Checked out of the gun room at 5451 Marathon for a gangster picture and never returned. The rental slip is still tucked under the stock, and the late fee now exceeds the picture's budget.

Thompson submachine gun with round drum magazine, walnut forward grip and stock, blued steel, folded blank paper tag tied to the trigger guard with string.

Tom Mix's Peacemaker tom_mix_s_peacemaker · weapon ranged

Tom Mix swears he only fired it for the cameras, the crowds at the Gower stables and one very insistent rattlesnake. The ivory grips have picked up a few new scratches he would rather not explain.

Long-barrelled single-action revolver, polished nickel finish with engraved scrollwork, carved white ivory grips, small silver star inlay, gleaming gold trigger.

Chandelier Chain chandelier_chain · weapon main

Links of a lobby chandelier's brass chain, wound around a lead counterweight. The hive had nested in the crystal; the chandelier came down, and the chain stayed on to be useful.

Heavy coil of ornate brass chain wrapped around a dull lead counterweight, one dangling cut-crystal prism, faint green-black chitin flecks caught in the links.

Post 43 Springfield post_43_springfield · weapon ranged

The men at Post 43 hand this rifle only to someone they would share a foxhole with, and they are particular. Its stock is scored with a tally that began in the Argonne and continues on Mount Lee.

Bolt-action service rifle, long dark walnut stock with rows of carved notches, blued barrel, olive canvas sling, small enamel blue-and-gold Legion star set into the butt.

Crawford's Thompson crawford_s_thompson · weapon ranged

Charlie Crawford keeps a violin case in his Sunset office that has never held a violin. When he opens it for someone, it means the syndicate trusts them, or wants them where the trouble is.

Thompson submachine gun with a gleaming round drum, gold-plated receiver, glossy rosewood grips, resting on an open black violin case lined with crimson velvet.

Letter H Girder letter_h_girder · weapon main

Torn from the HOLLYWOODLAND sign when the hive climbed Mount Lee, the steel still carries a strip of white sheet metal and a few dead bulbs. The realtors would like it back. The hive would like whoever is holding it.

Massive riveted steel girder used as a two-handed club, white-painted sheet metal panel bolted along one side, row of broken light bulbs, amber hive resin seeping from joints.

Hive Queen's Stinger hive_queen_s_stinger · weapon main

Taken from the queen of the Mount Lee Hive, who used it to decide which of the taken stayed awake for the cameras. Amber venom still beads at the tip, slow as honey and nowhere near as kind.

Long tapering stinger dagger, iridescent green-black chitin shaft, needle tip glowing with a bead of amber venom, base bound in pale silk-like hive fibre.

Tower Beacon tower_beacon · weapon off

Pulled from United Airport's control tower and rewired to run off a battery pack and sheer spite. It once guided mail planes over the Valley; now it guides the living out of the hangars.

Squat rotating aviation beacon lamp, red and clear glass halves in a brass cage, black iron base with heavy battery box, bright sweeping beam.

Architect's T-Square architect_s_t_square · weapon main

Belonged to the draughtsman who laid out the Lockheed plant, and later to the hive, which studied his plans with alarming interest. One edge is ruled in inches; the other in something no surveyor recognises.

Large steel T-square with polished walnut head, one edge etched with fine marks, opposite edge grown over with glossy green-black chitin ridges and faint amber veins.

Sister Aimee's Pulpit Bible sister_aimee_s_pulpit_bible · weapon off

Sister Aimee preached from this Bible to five thousand at Angelus Temple and to every radio in the basin over KFSG. The margins are crowded with her notes; the cover is scorched where it stopped something with too many legs.

Thick white leather Bible with gilt page edges, gold cross on the cover, scorched black patch on one corner, long blue silk ribbon marker hanging down.

Kop Captain's Nightstick kop_captain_s_nightstick · weapon main

Mack Sennett's Keystone captain waved it through two hundred chases and never hit anybody on purpose. The hive took over the studio and missed the joke entirely, so the stick has had a busier year.

Black lacquered police nightstick with a turned grip and leather wrist thong, small brass star-shaped shield badge nailed near the handle, scuffed chitin scratches along the shaft.

Hot Dog hot_dog · consumable

Bought from a cart at Hollywood and Highland whose owner refused to close for the end of the world. Mustard, onions and no questions.

A boiled frankfurter in a soft white bun with a bright yellow stripe of mustard and chopped onions, wrapped halfway in crinkled waxed paper.

Coffee coffee · consumable

Black, hot and boiling since dawn in an aid-station pot. Nobody drinks it for the taste.

A thick white enamel mug with a chipped dark blue rim, filled with steaming black coffee.

Ham Sandwich ham_sandwich · consumable

Rye bread, mustard and ham sliced thin enough to read a contract through. The studio commissary wraps them for extras who were told lunch was an hour ago.

A thick ham sandwich on brown rye cut diagonally, pink ham and green lettuce showing at the edge, one bite missing from a corner, resting on waxed paper.

Coca-Cola coca_cola · consumable

Ice cold from the drugstore cooler on Highland, where the soda jerk still counts the empties. No trucks get through the Dome, so every bottle opened is one fewer.

A curved pale green glass soda bottle holding dark brown cola, beaded with condensation, topped with a red crimped cap.

Blue Plate Special blue_plate_special · consumable

Meat loaf, mashed potatoes and peas sharing a divided plate at a Cahuenga lunch counter. The waitress calls everyone honey, the Legion sergeant included.

A blue-patterned divided china plate holding a slice of meat loaf under brown gravy, a scoop of mashed potatoes and a heap of green peas.

Hires Root Beer hires_root_beer · consumable

Sassafras, sarsaparilla and a foam head a temperance lady can admire in public. The Legion keeps a crate behind the bar it officially does not have.

A frosted heavy glass mug of dark root beer with a thick tan foam head spilling over the rim.

Chili Size chili_size · consumable

An open hamburger drowned in chili beans and onions, the house dish of every all-night stand south of Sunset. Eaten with a fork, a spoon and some resolve.

An oval white plate with a hamburger patty on an open bun buried under dark red chili beans, chopped white onions and grated orange cheese.

Egg Cream egg_cream · consumable

A New York soda-fountain drink with neither egg nor cream in it, brought west by a Brooklyn screenwriter who complains about the coast in every letter home.

A tall fluted soda glass of chocolate milk and seltzer under a frothy white head, a long steel spoon standing in it.

Cobb Salad cobb_salad · consumable

Chopped bacon, avocado, egg and chicken lined up in rows like a chorus line. A kitchen trick for clearing out the icebox, which under the Dome describes most of the menu.

A wide white bowl of chopped salad in neat stripes: green lettuce, yellow egg, red tomato, pale green avocado, crumbled bacon and blue cheese.

Orange Julep orange_julep · consumable

Valley oranges whipped frothy with ice at a stand on the road to Studio City. The groves beyond it are going to seed under the Dome, so the stand squeezes while the fruit lasts.

A tall paper cup of pale frothy orange drink with a red-striped straw and a bright orange wedge perched on the rim.

Musso & Frank Steak Dinner musso_frank_steak_dinner · consumable

Grilled over open flame on the Boulevard since 1919 and carried out by waiters who have outlasted three studio heads. They see no reason the insects should rate a hurry. Charge it to the picture.

A thick charred steak on a heavy white oval plate beside a split baked potato with a melting pat of butter and a sprig of green parsley.

Brown Derby Coffee brown_derby_coffee · consumable

Poured at the Brown Derby on Vine, where the cream comes in silver and the booths face the door so nobody misses an entrance. Agents drink it by the gallon and bill the client.

A fine white china cup and saucer with a thin brown rim stripe, steaming coffee inside, a small silver cream pitcher beside it.

Linen Bandage linen_bandage · consumable

Torn from bedsheets donated to the aid station by a Whitley Heights landlady who would like them back.

A loosely rolled strip of off-white torn linen with frayed edges, one end trailing free, a faint pink stain on the outer turn.

Johnson & Johnson Gauze Roll johnson_johnson_gauze_roll · consumable

Sterile, wrapped and sealed, shipped from New Jersey before the Dome came down and rationed ever since. Nurses count the rolls twice a shift.

A tight roll of white surgical gauze sliding half out of a blue-and-white paper wrapper marked with a plain red cross-shaped emblem.

Bootleg Gin bootleg_gin · consumable

Cut in an Edendale bathtub and bottled in whatever the drugstores threw out. Crawford's men swear it is genuine; the label swears it is hair tonic.

A clear medicine bottle of cloudy gin with a cork stopper, a twist of wire at the neck and a peeling blank paper label.

Cracked Mandible cracked_mandible · junk

Half a jaw that once worked through plaster, lath and a studio carpenter's lunch pail. The junkman on Cahuenga pays by the pound and asks no questions.

A single curved insect mandible, dull brown-black chitin with a jagged crack running down its serrated inner edge, tip chipped off.

Kinked at the middle like a rabbit-ear aerial after a bad night. Radio men swear these things hum on the KFSG frequency.

One long segmented insect antenna, glossy black, bent sharply at a right angle midway, fine bristles along its length, frayed at the snapped base.

Dull Chitin Flake dull_chitin_flake · junk

Too thin for armour, too ugly for jewellery. Somebody on the Boulevard is surely selling them as lucky charms.

A small flat shard of insect shell, matte greenish-grey with a faint oily sheen at one edge, irregular broken outline.

A clump of coyote coat, burrs included at no extra charge. The Gower Gulch saddlery won't touch it.

A tangled clump of tawny grey fur knotted around a couple of brown burrs and dried twigs, ragged at the edges.

Something gnawed it clean and then lost interest. Best not to wonder what it came from.

A short pale bone with knobbed ends, its shaft scored with tooth marks and one end splintered, yellowed and dusty.

Dry as the Volstead Act intended. It still smells of rye and poor decisions.

A curved pewter hip flask with a dented side and a screw cap hanging loose on its tiny chain, tarnished grey with scratches.

Broodling Husk broodling_husk · junk

The shed skin of a young one, light as tissue paper and still faintly warm. It crackles when picked up, which nobody enjoys.

A hollow translucent insect moult the size of a cat, amber-brown and papery, split open along its back, curled legs still attached.

Spitter acid ate a constellation of holes clean through it. Worth nothing, though it does make a fine cheese grater for the desperate.

A palm-sized oval scale of dark green-black chitin, riddled with small round corroded pits ringed in sickly yellow-green stains.

Half an admission to Grauman's Chinese, from the night before the Dome came down. Hell's Angels is still playing, which hardly seems fair.

Half of a small red paper admission ticket torn across the middle, ornate gold border, blank faded centre, creased and slightly curled.

Fender, bedspring and somebody's Model T door, beaten flat enough to carry. Every machine shop on the lot will take it, no questions about where it came from.

Warm to the touch long after it should have cooled, and something small is set inside it like a fly in amber. Men who dig it out of the Tunnels do not talk much afterward.
